115 年 9 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
9 月出口 872.2 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.9%，較上年
同月增 60.9%(按新臺幣計算增 68.2%)；第3 季出口 2,449.2 億美元，
較上年同季增 44.6%；累計 1 至 9 月 6,615.5 億美元，較上年同期增
46.2%(按新臺幣計算增 49.1%)。 
（二）進口 
9 月進口 635.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.8%，較上
年同月增 51.7%(按新臺幣計算增 58.6%)；第 3 季進口 1,818.0 億美
元，較上年同季增 44.3%；累計 1 至 9 月 5,010.0 億美元，較上年同
期增 41.3%(按新臺幣計算增 43.8%)。 
（三）出入超 
9 月出超 236.3 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 113.4 億美元；
累計 1 至 9 月出超 1,605.5 億美元，較上年同期增 625.4 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於創新科技應用商機持續擴展，推升相關供應鏈出貨動能，加上
消費性電子新品備貨需求與產品價格上漲效應之挹注， 9 月出口較
上年同月增 330.0 億美元(+60.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 391.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增
198.6 億美元(+1.0 倍)， 其中電腦及其附屬單元增157.4 億美元(+1.1
倍)、儲存媒體增 17.6 億美元(+2.2 倍)、交換器及路由器增 10.5
億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 311.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增97.1
億美元(+45.3%)，其中積體電路增 93.9 億美元(+46.1%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+33.7%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.8 億美元(+32.0%)。 
3. 礦產品出口 22.6 億美元，較上年同月增 10.5 億美元(+86.4%)，其
中石油煉製品增 10.1 億美元(+99.0%)。 
4. 機械出口 27.4 億美元(歷年單月次高)，較上年同月增 6.4 億美元
(+30.7%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.7 億美元(+61.0%)、未列名
具特殊功能機械增 0.6 億美元(+73.6%)。 
5. 基本金屬及其製品出口 26.9 億美元，較上年同月增 5.0 億美元
(+22.9%)，其中銅及其製品增 2.5 億美元(+65.1%)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.4 億美元(+11.7%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
1. 第 3 季出口較上年同季增 755.9 億美元(+44.6%)，以資通與視聽產
品增 367.6 億美元(+55.0%)、電子零組件增 308.4 億美元(+51.2%)
增加較多。 
2. 累計 1 至 9 月出口較上年同期增 2,089.8 億美元(+46.2%)，其中資
通與視聽產品增 1,222.2 億美元(+72.4%)、電子零組件增 726.6 億
美元(+45.7%)。

等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈全球分工與出口引申需求擴增，電子零組件、資通產品
及半導體設備進口維持強勁，加上國際農工原料行情處於高檔，9
月進口較上年同月增 216.7 億美元(+51.7%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口440.5 億美元， 較上年同月增163.9億美元(+59.3%)，
其中電子零組件增 112.6 億美元(+82.5%)、電腦之零附件增 19.4
億美元(+1.5 倍)、礦產品增 17.1 億美元(+38.9%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 128.0 億美元， 較上年同月增 36.0 億美元(+39.1%)，
其中資通設備增 18.4 億美元(+75.0%)、半導體設備增 12.4 億美元
(+34.9%)，交通運輸設備減 1.4 億美元(-46.4%)。 
3. 消費品進口 62.1 億美元，較上年同月增 16.5 億美元(+36.0%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.4 億美元(+5.3 倍)、筆電增 0.5 億
美元(+43.8%)，藥粧用品減 1.2 億美元(-16.6%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月對東協出口創新高，對美國、陸港為單月次高 
1. 對美國出口 274.6 億美元，較上年同月增 141.5 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 124.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)、 電子零組件增10.1
億美元(+1.3 倍)、機械增 2.2 億美元(+42.3%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 206.2 億美元，較上年同月增 47.8 億美元
(+30.2%)，其中電子零組件增 34.9 億美元(+30.5%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.7 億美元(+39.0%)、機械增 1.9 億美元(+43.7%)。 
3. 對東協出口 188.8 億美元，較上年同月增 91.2 億美元(+93.4%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 49.7 億美元(+2.2 倍)、電子零組件增 32.0
億美元(+63.3%)、礦產品增 4.8 億美元(+1.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 45.3 億美元，較上年同月增 7.7 億美元(+20.6%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 5.7 億美元(+34.1%)、電子零組件增 2.2 億美
元(+56.6%)，基本金屬及其製品減 0.8 億美元(-20.5%)。 
5. 對日本出口 30.7 億美元，較上年同月增 3.6 億美元(+13.4%)，其
中電子零組件增 1.5 億美元(+11.0%)、基本金屬及其製品增 0.6 億
美元(+32.5%)、機械增 0.5 億美元(+29.1%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月對美國出口 2,077.8 億美元，較上年同期增 773.1 億美
元(+59.3%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,600.9 億美元，增 355.3 億美
元(+28.5%)， 對東協出口1,339.6 億美元 ， 增463.6 億美元(+52.9%)，
對歐洲出口 421.8 億美元 ， 增138.4 億美元(+48.8%)， 對日本出口277.7
億美元，增 58.3 億美元(+26.6%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月自中國大陸與香港進口 135.0 億美元 ， 較上年同月增50.1 億美元
(+59.1%)，自南韓進口 116.3 億美元，增52.6 億美元(+82.5%)，自東
協進口 106.0 億美元，增 52.6 億美元(+98.4%)，自日本進口 64.3 億
美元，增 17.7 億美元(+38.0%)，自歐洲進口 50.2 億美元，增 3.5 億
美元(+7.4%)，自美國進口 48.1 億美元，增 6.5 億美元(+15.5%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增77.6%、
自南韓增 65.8%、自中國大陸與香港增 42.8%、自日本增 25.7%、自
美國增 21.4%、自歐洲增 10.8%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）主要國家/地區今年 1-8 月外貿景氣普遍走升，以南韓(1-9 月)出口年
增 56.8%最為強勁，香港、新加坡亦分別年增 49.0%、39.8%；中國
大陸、美國出口分別年增 19.3%及 14.6%，德國(1-7 月)、日本各增
9.7%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步上揚，累計 1-8 月以香港進口年增 50.5%較
突出，新加坡、中國大陸分別年增 37.3%、27.0%，南韓(1-9 月)、德
國(1-7 月)分別增 19.7%、10.0%，日本、美國各增 7.8%、4.2%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續擴展，
加以步入傳統外銷旺季 、產品價格上漲效應，9 月出口 872.2 億美元，
年增 60.9%，連續 35 個月正成長；進口635.9 億美元，年增51.7%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口引申需求擴張，及國際原物料行情居
高；出、進口值及出超 236.3 億美元齊創單月新高。累計 1 至 9 月出
口年增 46.2%、進口增 41.3%。
（二）9 月主要貨品出口普遍較上年同月成長，以資通與視聽產品增 1.0 倍
最佳，包括顯示卡、伺服器、儲存媒體、 交換器及路由器等出口均
呈倍數增加 ；其次為礦產品增 86.4%，主因全球油品供應偏緊支撐價
格維持高檔；電子零組件隨 AI 應用及消費性電子新品備貨需求升溫，
增 45.3%，機械、電機產品亦各增 3 成上下。累計 1 至 9 月資通與視
聽產品、電子零組件出口併計增 59.5%，占總出口比重 79.0%。

（三）主要市場方面，9 月對美國、東協出口值分創歷年單月次高、新高，
年增率各為 1.1 倍及 93.4%，合計對總出口增加貢獻達 7 成；對中國
大陸與香港、歐洲、日本出口各年增 30.2%、20.6%、13.4%。累計 1
至 9 月對五大市場出口值皆為歷年同期最高，其中對美國、東協均
提前刷新全年紀錄 ， 分別年增59.3%、52.9%，對歐洲出口年增48.8%，
對陸港、日本各增 28.5%、26.6%。
（四）展望未來，全球貿易政策及地緣政治情勢變化等，仍增添總體 經濟
不確定性，惟隨人工智慧應用 持續深化，國際雲端服務業者 及各國
加速 AI 基礎建設投資，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供應鏈需求擴增，
加以科技新品備貨、中國大陸及歐美年底採購旺季將屆， 均有利挹
注我國外銷動能，第 4 季出口可望延續成長態勢。

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。 
図表 1 各月次経済指標の推移

（注）青字は改善、赤字は悪化を示す

日本銀行「消費活動指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 2

2

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

ト調査」等より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

けて～」 、内閣府経済社会総合研究所「経済分析」第208号

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

## Document 148: 261030_ubs_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_ubs_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 30 October 2026
Global Research
Key Call: MediaTek Inc.
Raising TPU guidance with stronger execution
TPU v9 on track to ramp from early 2028
Amid market skepticism on TPU execution, MediaTek delivered better outlook for Q4
and Cloud ASICs, as we previewed. Management raised TPU sales guidance from >US
$2bn to US$2.5bn for 2026, and from US$12-16bn to US$16bn for 2027. This reflects
the steady Google demand for v8t for training, critical to accelerate the new Gemini LLM

development. Importantly, for TPU v9, MediaTek re-affirmed it is on track for high
volume manufacturing in Q128, which clarified  recent noises around delayed tape out.
Our latest understanding is there may be two versions of MediaTek’s TPU v9: the first
version with 336G SerDes should be tape out soon in Nov 2026. There’s a second tape
out which is on track for delivery in Feb 2027. The second version features flexible
SerDes switch between 224G/336G for greater supply flexibility for the whole system.

Conclusion is, we think MediaTek is on good track to scale TPU  sales to our estimates of
US$44bn/53bn in 2028/29  with upside. See Figure 1U B S  f o r e c a s t s  f o r   M e d i a T e k ' s  T P U  b u s i n e s s for our TPU financial analysis.
Thoughts on the second Cloud ASIC client
MediaTek announced the 2nd Cloud ASIC client, which should contribute to revenue in
2028. MediaTek clarified it’s not a Chinese customer. We believe there are 2 higher

possibilities as what we flagged recently. 1) Tesla’s AI6 using Intel 14A, produced at
Terafab ramping in H228. Potential revenue opportunity on full year basis could be ~US
$2bn if MediaTek could ramp 1m units. Margin profile could be lower than TPU's 42%
GM given simpler design. 2) SpaceX/xAI's Dojo3. MediaTek should be partnering with
Nvidia to offer NVLink Fusion for faster time to market. Question is SpaceX/xAI's own
engineering resource and capability for Dojo3 after team reshuffle. If secured, our initial

estimate is revenue opportunity a year could be US$4-9bn, assuming good volume.
Margin profile may be similar or higher than TPU, in our view.
Meaningful operating margin expansion ahead
Q4 sales are guided to grow 28% QoQ at the mid point, beating consensus of 25%,
even with higher base from Q3. GM is guided to edge down to 45.0% at mid point, with
OPM meaningfully rising to 19.0% from 16.1% in Q3. As MediaTek starts ramping TPU
substantially into 2027, the key focus for profitability should shift from GM to OPM due

to different business model vs consumer electronics, and our sense is the market still
under appreciates the structural shift and the stronger earnings power. We forecast
MediaTek's OPM to rise to 25.2%/30.9% in 2027/28 vs consensus of 21.8%/24.2%.
Our 2027/28E EPS estimates are 23%/29% above consensus pre earnings.
Valuation: maintain Key Call Buy and target of NT$7,300
Among our coverage, MediaTek remains the one that we believe the market most

underestimates its execution and opportunity in Cloud AI. We raise 2026E EPS by 5%
and fine tune 2027/28E EPS by 1-2%. Maintain Key Call Buy. Our target of NT$7,300 is
based on 27x 2027-28E PE.

Company Description
MediaTek is a leading integrated circuit (IC) design company in Taiwan, focusing on solutions
from the edge to the cloud, applications for its product offerings range from smartphones,
smart homes and AI PCs to high-performance computing, automotive, and AI data centers.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for MediaTek is based on a target PE methodology.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights. In
addition, MediaTek is exposed to potential price competition in handset SoC, TV SoC, etc.

Downside risks include slower-than-expected market share gains and a weaker-than-
expected demand recovery.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 2

2

耕興為台灣電子產品測試及驗證服務龍頭廠商：耕興成立於 1986 年，以強制性檢
測服務起家 ， 目前為台灣最大民營電子產品測試及驗證服務實驗室 ， 集檢測 、 認證 、
技術服務與零件銷售為一體，提供電子產品 EMC/Safety 等專業認證及防磁零組
件銷售，為全球少數能執行 5G sub-6GHz (FR1)、5G mmWave (FR2)、Wi-Fi 6、
Wi-Fi 6E高速行動通訊 、 無線傳輸產品且第一家取得802.11ay 與 5G NR mmWave
產品檢測認證之公司，並於台灣 /大陸/美國/德國設立實驗室。以營收結構區分，
檢測與認證收入佔營收約 90%、被動元件銷售佔 10%。國際競爭對手包含
UL/BV/PCTEST/SGS/Intertek 等、地區型競爭對手為耀登/東研信超/華測檢測/譜
尼測試等。 
2Q26 財報優於預期：耕興 2Q26 EPS 3.07 元，優於預期的 2.87 元，5G 手機、
AI NB、 企業AP 等產品檢測訂單狀況維持穩定 ， 公司1H26 5G NR市佔率 23.97%、
FCC 高階網通市占率 47.3%，維持領導地位。 
3Q26 FCC 轉單效益開始小幅貢獻：由於 FCC 擬禁止中國實驗室驗證賣往美國的
產品，公司開始收到一些高階測試的轉單，但 FCC 尚未正式全面禁止，故現在貢
獻不多，仍在等FCC 正式決策，預計11 月會有結果，目前粗估若轉單順利會有共
2~6 億元的營收貢獻，整體 3Q26 營運平穩， 預估 3Q26 營收為 12.47 億元
(+0.4%QoQ，+10.7%YoY)，毛利率 46.8%，營業利益率 30.5%，稅後淨利 2.93
億元(-6.3%QoQ，+13.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.88 元。

### Page 3

3

2027 年公司若受益轉單，營運有望小幅創高：FCC 未來擬禁止所有中國實驗室為
在美國使用的電子設備 (手機、PC 等) 進行測試，研究處認為2027 年開始公司有
望受益中國實驗室受限產生的轉單效應，公司在 2027 年已有擴產計畫，地點仍待
公告，若 NTN (非地面網路)需求如預期增加，有相關設備購買需求，明年 CAPEX
有望上看 4 億台幣，整體預期營運可小幅創高，預估 2026 年營收為 49.04 億元
(+9.1%YoY)， 毛利率46.7%， 營業利益率29.3%， 稅後淨利11.39 億元(+3.9%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 11.18 元，2027 年營收為 52.04 億元(+6.1%YoY)，毛利率 47.4%，營
業利益率 31.2%，稅後淨利 12.54 億元(+10.1%YoY)，稅後 EPS 12.31 元，以過
往現金股利配發率 80%來算，預計配發 9.0 元現金股利，換算目前殖利率 4.2%，
維持買進建議，目標價 246 元(20X 2027 EPS)。

## Document 198: [000270] 기아(000270) 3Q26 Review_ EV 판매 증가의 역풍기아(000270) 3Q26 Review_ EV 판매 증가의 역풍3Q26 Review_ EV 판매 증가의 역풍 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 3Q26 Review_ EV 판매 증가의 역풍기아(000270) 3Q26 Review_ EV 판매 증가의 역풍3Q26 Review_ EV 판매 증가의 역풍 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(000270) 
기아 
2026.11.02

이재일 CFA 
6183 / lee.jae-il@eugenefn.com

3Q26 Review: EV 판매 증가의 역풍 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 160,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 95,600원(10/30)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1%yoy), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%yoy, OPM 5.8%), 지배이익 1.29조원(-9.8%yoy)을 기록, 도매 판
매 증가로 인한 매출 증가에도 불구하고 인센티브/원자재 비용 증가로 인해 
컨센서스를 하회하는 실적 쇼크를 기록 
 영업이익 증감 요인: 인센티브&가격 -7,270억원, 믹스 -4,650억원, 재료비 
-3,680억원으로 총 1.56조원 비용 발생. 증가 요인은 판매량 +4,420억원, 
환율 +5,290억원, 기타비용 절감 +4,130억원으로 1.38조원 상쇄. 인센티
브로 인한 비용 증가 예상외로 컸는데 EV 판매량 증가 영향. 기말환율 하락
으로 판매보증충당금은 전년비 9,254억원 감소 
 전동화 믹스 증가: 글로벌 xEV 소매 판매 30만대(+47%yoy), 판매비중 
36.2%(+9.8%p yoy). HEV 17.2만대(+46%yoy), 텔루라이드, 스포티지 
HEV 공급 확대로 3분기 미국 HEV 판매 +118%yoy 기록. EV 12.3만대
(+75%yoy), 서유럽 EV 판매는 +163%yoy 기록 
 4분기 전망: 중동 분쟁 지속으로 원가 부담의 급격한 개선은 어려울 전망. 
연초 CID에서 제시한 연간 판매 목표는 달성 가능할 것으로 보이지만 기존 
수익성 목표를 하회할 전망. 중장기적으로 상품 경쟁력 강화, 지역별 파워트
레인 대응 전략 유지, 내부 원가 및 체질 개선으로 외부 비용 부담 상쇄를 
추진할 계획. 중동 분쟁 완화에 따른 비용 부담 해소와 HEV 비중 확대를 통
한 수익성 개선은 2027년 시점이 될 것으로 예상 
 단기 방향 전환 어려울 듯: 판매 호조에도 불구하고 저가형 EV 판매 비중 
증가함에 따라 수익성이 악화되는 모습이 3분기에도 재확인. 글로벌 수요 
변화를 감안했을 때, EV 시장 선점이 매우 중요한 것은 사실이지만 단기 수
익성 부담 커지고 있는 점 우려됨. 중국산 EV의 글로벌 확산, 중동 전쟁 장
기화에 따른 원가 부담 증대가 연말까지는 영향을 미칠 것. 동사에 대한 투
자의견 매수와 목표주가 16만원 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

## Document 216: [005380] 현대차(005380) 생산 차질의 이연이 내년 성장으로현대차(005380) 생산 차질의 이연이 내년 성장으로생산 차질의 이연이 내년 성장으로 - 기업

주가 추이

3Q26 매 출액 과 영 업 이익 은 비현 금 성 비용 항목 개 선 으로 시장 기대치
를 상 회 했 으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인 플 레 이션 등으로 연간 가이 던 스 는 
하 향 되 었 습니 다. 올해 생산 차질 에 따라 이 연된 신차 판 매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시 화 하 는 부분 에 주목 필 요 합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 Y oY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제 외 하 면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년 으 로 이연된 것 이 라 는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본 격 화 된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로 컬 업 체 들 의 공세를 방 어 하 는 모 습 을 
확인하는 것이 가 능 할 것으로 판 단 한 다. 노 후 차 종 중심 운 영 에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근 원 적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 30일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 232: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

시가총액(십억원) 39,188

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

## Document 241: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% Y oY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% Y oY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다. 
목표주가 (유지) 2,000,000원  
현재가 (10/21) 1,417,000원

## Document 266: AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

德意志银⾏警告， AI 泡沫若破裂，冲击可能从美股蔓延⾄美元。外国投资者持有逾 20 万亿
美元美国股票，若股市、美元与美国实际利率同步下⾏，传统汇率对冲机制或将失效， AI
抛售潮可能演变为美元危机。
摘要：
当 AI 泡沫开始消退，最⼤的⻛险可能不是纳斯达克回调，⽽是美元崩塌。外国投资者持有逾 20
万亿美元的美国股票资产，⼀旦 AI 驱动的抛售引发股市与美元同步下跌，市场将⾯临远超技术性
修正的宏观冲击。
德意志银⾏警告， AI 抛售潮可能触发美国股市、货币对冲与美元之间的危险反馈循环。与以往科
技股回调不同，这⼀次美元的传统避险属性正受到根本质疑 —— 强美元未必能再充当外国投资
者的损失缓冲垫。
如果纳斯达克、美元指数（ DXY ）与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，市场正在定价的将不仅是仓
位的技术性调整，⽽是对整个 AI 繁荣周期的系统性重估。这意味着下⼀轮 AI 抛售，有可能演变为
⼀场美元危机。
20 万亿美元的脆弱敞⼝
据德意志银⾏估算，外国投资者持有的美国股票资产中，约 90% 的价值来⾃累积的价格升值，

⽽⾮实际净购⼊。换⾔之，这⼀庞⼤头⼨⾼度依赖美国股市估值的持续维持。
更关键的是，外国股票资⾦流⼊⽬前已承担了美国经常账⼾⾚字的全部融资需求，美元因此与
股票市场周期深度绑定。⼀旦股市出现⼤幅调整，美元的资⾦来源将直接承压。
对冲机制在美国本⼟冲击下失效
过去⼗年，每当美国股市下跌，美元⾛强往往帮助外国投资者对冲了部分损失。但这⼀保护机
制有⼀个前提：冲击来⾃外部。若⻛险源头在美国本⾝，股市与美元可能同向下跌，对冲逻辑
随之⽡解。
StoneX 的数据显⽰， AI 情绪与美元之间的五⽇滚动相关系数⾼达 +0.97 ，⽽ 20 ⽇相关系数仅为
+0.31 。这意味着短期内两者已形成⾼度同步 ——AI 信⼼⼀旦动摇，美元将同步承压。
对于未对冲的外国投资者⽽⾔，股票和汇率的双重亏损将造成双重打击。即便他们不抛售股
票，单纯提⾼货币对冲⽐例的操作本⾝，也会形成额外的抛售美元压⼒，加剧汇率下⾏。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

实际利率是关键验证信号
德意志银⾏认为， AI 资本开⽀预期的急速膨胀，是推动美国股市与实际利率同步⾛⾼的重要驱动
⼒之⼀。因此，实际利率的⾛向将成为判断市场性质的关键信号。
若科技股、美元与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，将表明投资者开始折现⼀个更疲弱的 AI 投资周
期 —— 这将把⼀次技术性股市修正，升级为更⼴泛的宏观重新定价。相反，若实际利率维持⾼
位，则调整更可能只是仓位层⾯的波动。
做空美元或成 AI 修正的隐性交易
⾯对 AI 潜在回调，最直观的交易是做空科技股。但德意志银⾏指出，不那么显眼、却可能更具威
⼒的交易是做空美元。
⼀旦以美国为中⼼的股票冲击成真，外国投资者将被迫重新审视其在这 20 万亿美元资产上的货
币对冲策略，传统的股票 - 美元正相关关系可能发⽣逆转。投资者需要同时盯紧纳斯达克、 DXY
与美国实际利率三个指标。若三者共同⾛低，市场所定价的⻛险将远⼤于⼀次普通的仓位出清
—— 下⼀轮 AI 抛售，或将以⼀场美元抛售收场。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 272: Anthropic IPO前更新招股书凸显盈利压力：Q3营收近170亿美元、同比增11倍，净亏损扩大92%，算力承诺近9000亿

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO前更新招股书凸显盈利压力：Q3营收近170亿美元、同比增11倍，净亏损扩大92%，算力承诺近9000亿.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

新招股书显⽰，今年前三季度， Anthropic 营收超 330 亿美元、同⽐增 12 倍；三季度运营费
⽤逾 175 亿美元、超过营收，同⽐增 2.9 倍，其中算⼒及基础设施⽀出增 3.8 倍⾄ 98.6 亿，同
⽐增速远超⼆季度的 48% ；三季度经营业绩由⼆季度盈利 9.7 亿美元转亏 5.67 亿；到三季度
末承诺⽀出 8950 亿美元，其中 4475 亿在 2030 年底前发⽣，将近 700 亿需此后⼀年内⽀付。
摘要：
Anthropic IPO 前更新的招股书显⽰，尽管 Claude ⺟公司营收继续⾼速增⻓，但算⼒成本和⻓期
基础设施投⼊也在急剧膨胀，第三季度经营业绩由盈转亏、净亏损同⽐扩⼤，近 9000 亿美元的
算⼒及基础设施承诺⽀出进⼀步凸显其盈利压⼒。
根据 Anthropic 周⼀向美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）提交的修订版 S-1 招股书，公司第三季度营收
达到 169.56 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 1127% ；净亏损达到 232.91 亿美元，较上年同期的 121.38 亿美元

扩⼤约 92% 。截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇及此后，公司已签订的计算能⼒采购及租赁协议承诺⽀出总额约为
8954 亿美元。
企业客⼾持续扩张，前三季度营收超 330 亿美元
Anthropic 第三季度营收达到 169.56 亿美元，较第⼆季度的 115.55 亿美元环⽐增⻓约 46.7% ，较
2025 年同期的 13.82 亿美元增⻓ 1127% 。这意味着公司单季营收已达到⼀年前的约 12.3 倍。
从前三季度整体表现来看， Anthropic 今年前九个⽉累计营收达到 332.42 亿美元，远⾼于去年同
期的 25.45 亿美元，增幅约为 12 倍。企业客⼾的持续扩张是推动收⼊增⻓的重要动⼒。
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Anthropic 过去 12 个⽉贡献⾄少 10 万美元收⼊的企业客⼾超过 1.19 万家，其中，
过去 12 个⽉贡献⾄少 100 万美元收⼊的客⼾超过 1900 家，贡献⾄少 1000 万美元收⼊的客⼾约
250 家。
不过，营收⾼速增⻓的同时， Anthropic 的净亏损也在扩⼤。第三季度净亏损为 232.91 亿美元，

同⽐扩⼤约 92% ，较第⼆季度净亏损额 663.09 亿美元环⽐收窄空昂孔明 65% ，但主要受到可转
换票据、衍⽣负债及 SAFE 协议公允价值变动等⾮现⾦项⽬影响，不能简单理解为公司核⼼经营
亏损同步收窄。
算⼒成本激增，经营业绩较⼆季度由盈转亏
营收增⻓的另⼀⾯，是 Anthropic 为训练和运⾏ AI 模型付出的成本迅速攀升。
修订版招股书显⽰， Anthropic 第三季度总运营费⽤达到 175.23 亿美元，较上年同期的 44.71 亿美
元增⻓约 292% ，已经超过当季 169.56 亿美元的营收。
其中，算⼒及基础设施⽀出达到 98.63 亿美元，较上年同期的 20.47 亿美元增⻓约 382% ，相当于
当季营收的约 58% 。相⽐之下，这⼀⽐例在第⼆季度约为 48% ，显⽰算⼒投⼊加速正在给利润
率带来压⼒。
受成本上升影响， Anthropic 第三季度经营亏损达到 5.67 亿美元，⽽第⼆季度公司曾实现 9.74 亿
美元经营利润。这也是公司在连续多个季度经营亏损后，于第⼆季度⾸次实现经营盈利，随后

⼜在第三季度重新陷⼊经营亏损。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Anthropic 解释称，第三季度经营业绩由盈转亏，主要受到两⽅⾯因素影响：⼀是约 50 亿美元通
过短期承诺采购的算⼒成本，这类算⼒⽬前明显贵于多年期合同采购的同类算⼒；⼆是约 20 亿
美元与员⼯捐赠匹配计划有关的⾮现⾦费⽤。
不过，剔除股权激励、⾮现⾦慈善捐赠等项⽬后， Anthropic 第三季度调整后经营利润仍达到
24.63 亿美元，⾼于第⼆季度的 22.35 亿美元。但调整后经营利润率从第⼆季度的 19% 降⾄第三
季度的 15% ，反映出更昂贵的短期算⼒采购仍在侵蚀公司的利润空间。
这也意味着， Anthropic 虽然已经证明了 AI 产品具备强劲的商业化能⼒，但能否将营收增⻓持续
转化为经营利润，仍取决于算⼒成本下降的速度能否跟上业务扩张。
近 9000 亿美元算⼒承诺，近半将在 2030 年底前兑现
⽐单季度成本更引⼈关注的，是 Anthropic 已经锁定的⻓期算⼒和基础设施⽀出。
修订版招股书披露，截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇及此后，公司签订的算⼒采购承诺和租赁协议总额约为 8954 亿

美元，其中绝⼤部分与计算能⼒及基础设施有关。
从⽀出时间表来看，约 4475 亿美元预计将在 2030 年 12 ⽉ 31 ⽇前兑现，近半数承诺⽀出将在未来
四年多内陆续落地；另有约 698 亿美元将在截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇后的 12 个⽉内、即 2027 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇之前
⽀付。其余绝⼤部分承诺预计将在 2031 年⾄ 2045 年间兑现。
Anthropic 表⽰，随着计算需求⼤幅增⻓，公司正在从单纯依赖固定期限云计算协议，转向结合
直接租赁芯⽚、专⽤数据中⼼等⽅式的混合模式，希望通过建设⾃有专⽤基础设施，逐步以成
本更低的⻓期算⼒替代⾼价短期采购。
不过，这些承诺也意味着巨⼤的资⾦压⼒。公司在招股书中警告，部分算⼒采购协议包含最低
采购承诺和照付不议条款，即使实际使⽤量不⾜，公司仍可能需要⽀付相关费⽤。
更值得注意的是， Anthropic 的收⼊主要依赖客⼾实际使⽤量，并⾮全部来⾃⻓期合同承诺；⽽
算⼒和基础设施⽀出却往往需要提前多年锁定。如果客⼾需求增⻓不及预期，或者公司⽆法及

时将新增算⼒转化为收⼊，可能⾯临现⾦流压⼒，甚⾄难以履⾏相关合同义务。
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Anthropic 拥有约 1466 亿美元流动性，包括 956 亿美元现⾦及现⾦等价物、 363 亿
美元短期投资，以及 148 亿美元信贷额度可⽤资⾦。公司表⽰，未来将通过业务收⼊和融资筹集
资⾦⽀持相关投资。
IPO 估值⾯临重新审视， AI 商业模式接受考验
Anthropic 此次更新招股书，正值其筹备潜在的重磅 IPO 之际。
公司已于 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇⾸次公开提交招股书，计划在纳斯达克全球精选市场上市，股票代码为
ANT 。本次修订⽂件补充了第三季度财务数据，也进⼀步披露了公司的算⼒采购和⻓期⽀出安
排。
据此前报道， Anthropic 有望在美国感恩节假期前完成 IPO ，融资规模可能⽐肩甚⾄超过 SpaceX
今年 6 约创纪录的 862 亿美元 IPO 融资。⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利担任此次 IPO 的牵头承销商，另有 20 家
银⾏参与。
然⽽，随着最新财务数据披露，投资者对 Anthropic 估值的判断也开始出现变化。

IN THIS ISSUE
Macro Strategy—Guide to El Niño: What It Is, Why It Matters, and What to Do
About It: As crude oil flirts with April’s highs, and the price of diesel sits comfortably
above $6 per gallon, disruption to the global energy complex has emerged as a major
theme of 2026. Add yet another variable to the equation: the current “El Niño” event,

on track to be one of the strongest on record. The upshot: more extreme weather
from droughts to heavy rains, warmer temperatures globally, lower crop production in
commodity-exporting economies, and higher prices across major agricultural products.
Importantly, these pressures won’t be equally distributed and will largely depend on the
magnitude and timing of weather events. Among the most vulnerable will be heavily
import-dependent countries in the Middle East, Africa and parts of Asia, while developed

economies that count food as a smaller share of consumer spending should be better
insulated. For investors, the prospect of a very strong El Niño only further reinforces our
conviction in several structural trends including a return to higher inflation and higher
interest rates, elevated commodity prices amid heightened geopolitical risk and supply
chain shortages, and greater investment in energy infrastructure and supply chain
resilience. In portfolios, we favor commodities as both an inflation hedge and diversifier.

Market View—Another Tale of Market Rotation: October offered another reminder
that headline index returns do not always tell the full story. The S&P 500 ended the month
slightly lower, but the more muted headline return masked meaningful shifts in market
leadership beneath the surface. At the center of the volatility was a sharp unwind in the
Artificial Intelligence (AI) trade, particularly across semiconductors and memory stocks. Yet
pressure on AI market leaders has not translated into a broad retreat from U.S. Equities.

Instead, capital has moved toward previously lagging areas of the market, with Value-
oriented, defensive and rate-sensitive stocks gaining ground. While it remains too early to
determine whether the shift will prove durable, October’s rotation may reflect less a retreat
from the AI theme and more a recalibration of valuations and expected returns as market
leadership broadens. For investors, the speed of the shift reinforces the importance of
diversification as the bull market enters a potentially more mature and differentiated phase.

Thought of the Week—Reorienting Around AI: From Corporate Plans to Construction
to Capital Goods: AI is reshaping the physical economy, and companies are reorienting
capacity and investment plans to meet surging demand for data centers, power and
infrastructure. Industrial firms are adding or reviving capacity, while data center construction
has increased more than 60% over the past year and is outpacing other major construction
categories. At the same time, robust capital goods orders and long lead times for inputs such

as generators, switchboards, chillers and transformers highlight both the strength of demand
and the supply constraints the buildout faces. Taking stock of these signposts, our view
is reinforced that the AI investment cycle remains in its middle innings, creating potential
opportunities for companies supplying the equipment and infrastructure behind the buildout.

### Page 2

2 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Guide to El Niño: What It Is, Why It Matters, and What to Do About It
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
As crude oil flirts with April’s highs, and the price of diesel sits comfortably above $6 per
gallon, disruption to the global energy complex has emerged as a major theme of 2026.
Add yet another variable to the equation: the current “El Niño” event, on track to be one

of the strongest on record. Below we answer frequently asked questions on El Niño,
the potential impact on the global economy and markets and, most importantly, how
investors can position portfolios accordingly.
What is El Niño, and what makes this time different? El Niño is the warm phase
of the “El Niño-Southern Oscillation,” a natural climate pattern occurring every two to
seven years. Simply put, El Niño refers to the warming of sea surface temperatures in

the eastern Pacific, historically leading to drier conditions in some areas (Southeast Asia,
Australia, Central America, southern Africa) and wetter conditions in others (southern
U.S., parts of Latin America) (Exhibit 1).
What makes this episode different? Should forecasts prove correct, this El Niño is on
track to be historic. The National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration’s latest
outlook assigns at least an 83% probability that El Niño’s strength during October-

December will exceed that of any previous episode since 1950. Already, its impact has
been felt via wildfires in Indonesia, the weakest monsoon season in India since 2015, 1
and severe flooding from Paraguay to Peru. The upshot: more extreme weather, hotter
temperatures globally, lower crop production in commodity-exporting economies, and
higher prices across major agricultural products.
Exhibit 1: El Niño's Impact Varies Globally.
Geography Typical rainfall pattern Specific impacts

South Asia Weaker summer monsoon risk Lower crop yields, water shortages, food inflation
Southeast Asia Drier Lower rice and palm oil production, wildfire risk, food inflation
United States Wetter in the south; warmer/drier in the north Southern flood risk; lower northern heating demand
Central America & Northern South America Drier Drought, lower crop yields, water shortages, food inflation, lower hydropower output
Southern Africa Drier Weaker maize harvests, food inflation, lower hydropower output

Western South America Wetter along the Pacific coast Flooding, infrastructure damage, disrupted fisheries
Southern South America Wetter Flooding and crop damage; potential yield gains in some areas
Australia Drier Drought, agricultural losses, elevated wildfire risk, food inflation
Source: National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration, World Meteorological Organization, Food and Agriculture Organization, Australian Bureau of Meteorology, World Bank. Data as of

October 30, 2026.
How will El Niño impact the global economy and markets? Which regions will be
most affected? El Niño’s impact on the global economy and markets is likely to be felt
most via commodity price inflation. Alongside higher energy and fertilizer costs resulting
from war in Europe and the Middle East, El Niño could put further upward pressure on
commodities like wheat, sugar, corn, soy, cocoa and coffee as drought (or extreme rain)
weighs on crop yields and production. Latest estimates from the United Nations Food

and Agriculture Organization (FAO) suggest global food prices were up 5.8% year-over-
year (YoY) in September (Exhibit 2). Livestock is also likely to be challenged by higher
temperatures globally, while flooding has already threatened copper production in
Chile. Meanwhile, with hydropower accounting for more than half of the average annual
electricity generation in Brazil, Colombia, Peru and other Latin American countries, 2
below-average rainfall in affected areas has huge implications for electricity generation.

As an example, Colombia’s natural gas demand nearly doubled during the 2023-2024 El
Niño event.2 Another key watchpoint will be whether drier conditions reduce water levels
1 India Meteorological Department, Bloomberg. Refers to rainfall between June and September as of September 30, 2026.
2 International Energy Agency, as of October 7, 2026
Investment Implications
We expect El Niño to place upward
pressure on agricultural goods
prices globally. We continue to

see commodities as a strategic
diversifier and hedge against inflation
in portfolios. The U.S. remains our
preferred region with companies in
Industrials and Materials as potential
beneficiaries. Meanwhile, economic
impacts to the emerging world will vary
by country but the broader Emerging
Market (EM) equity market could be
less exposed given its significant tilt
toward Technology.

### Page 3

3 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
in the Panama Canal and other key shipping routes, particularly in light of the Strait of
Hormuz closure.
These pressures won’t be equally distributed and will heavily depend on this El Niño’s
severity. Among the more vulnerable to higher agricultural prices will be heavily import-
dependent countries in the Middle East, Africa, and parts of Asia. So too will economies
that count food as a larger share of consumer spending such as the Philippines (36%),

Indonesia (33%), Egypt (33%), India (28%), and Vietnam (26%) (Exhibit 3). Developed
economies like the U.S. (6%), Canada (10%), and Germany (11%) stand to be better
insulated from a food-related shock to headline inflation.
Within the U.S., some impacts could be positive with a potentially milder winter reducing
heating costs and wetter conditions in the South improving agricultural conditions. A recent
paper from the Federal Reserve Bank of San Francisco found that, in addition to putting

upward pressure on personal consumption expenditures (PCE) inflation, strong El Niño
episodes have been historically associated with a temporary boost to employment in the
following year via better construction conditions in the North and weather-event recovery
efforts.3 In California and parts of the Southwest, El Niño will be yet another stress test for
property insurers as flooding and landslide risks increase.
For more vulnerable developing economies, meanwhile, higher food and energy costs could

present a policy dilemma. On one hand, governments in vulnerable areas may move to
introduce fiscal relief to support households and boost growth. History points to plenty of
examples of this, such as Indonesia’s cash assistance in late 2023 or Thailand’s $1.8 billion
in loans to farmers in 2015. On the other hand, elevated inflation expectations and, in
some cases, weaker currencies could limit EM central banks’ room to ease monetary policy,
particularly with several major developed central banks aligned in hiking interest rates. Also

complicating matters: Global sovereign debt surpassed $110 trillion in Q2 2026, leaving
less fiscal space to absorb incoming shocks. 4
How can investors position portfolios accordingly? In many ways, the prospect of a
very strong El Niño only reinforces structural trends we’ve identified as defining the coming
years: a return to higher inflation and higher interest rates; elevated commodity prices
amid heightened geopolitical risk and resource/supply shortages; and greater investment in

energy infrastructure and supply chain resilience.
Considering these themes, we continue to like commodities not just as a hedge against
higher inflation but also as a diversification tool in portfolios given their negative correlation
with Equities and bonds. That’s not to mention secular forces placing upward pressure on
commodity prices such as the resource-intensive AI buildout and broader protectionism. To
wit, the Bloomberg Commodity Index, which spans oil, gold, various metals and agricultural

goods has gained nearly 30% this year, handily outperforming the S&P 500 (+1%) and the
Bloomberg U.S. Aggregate Bond Index (-1%).
Within Equities, the U.S. is still our preferred region. While upside risk to inflation remains
a risk to watch going into 2027, food is a relatively small share of the consumer basket in
the U.S., and neither strong services spending nor capital investment should be materially
affected by El Niño. Companies within the Industrials and Materials sectors focused on

precision agriculture, water management and crop protection may be positioned to benefit.
Import-dependent EM economies remain particularly exposed, though impacts will vary
widely depending on the magnitude and timing of weather events. It’s worth noting too that
EM Equities remain heavily concentrated in East Asian markets with substantial exposure to
technology, leaving the broader index arguably less exposed to the economic ramifications
of El Niño than in past episodes. Indeed, even after recent weakness in semiconductor

names, Taiwan, Korea, and China together account for a near-70% of the broader MSCI
Emerging Markets Index. 5 We maintain a slight overweight to the region in portfolios given
earnings strength but favor an active approach.

4 Institute of International Finance, as of September 23, 2026.
5 FactSet, as of October 27, 2026.

### Page 4

4 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Another Tale of Market Rotation
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
October offered another reminder that headline index returns do not always tell the
full story. The S&P 500 ended the month slightly lower, down almost 1%, but the more
muted headline return masked meaningful shifts in market leadership beneath the
surface. At the center of the volatility was a sharp unwind in the AI trade, particularly

across semiconductors and memory stocks, with the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index
experiencing a 19% intramonth drawdown. The dominant concerns in recent months
have centered on the sustainability of AI infrastructure spending and whether the scale
of investment will ultimately generate sufficient returns. Those only intensified amid a
sharp rise in bond yields after the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield spiked above 5.3% as of
October 5 for the first time since 2002, raising scrutiny over AI financing conditions and

whether higher borrowing costs could increase hurdle rates for continued investment
and slow the pace of capital spending.
Yet the pressure on AI market leaders has not translated into a broad retreat from U.S.
Equities. Instead, capital has moved toward previously lagging areas of the market,
with Value-oriented, defensive and rate-sensitive stocks gaining ground. Value defeated
Growth in October, with the Russell 1000 Value Index outperforming its Growth

counterpart by 10 percentage points, an outcome seen in only about 1% of months since
1979. The move extends Value's outperformance for the year, with the index up 23%
year-to-date compared with down almost 1% for Growth. Participation also improved.
The equal-weighted S&P 500 outperformed its market-cap-weighted counterpart by its
widest monthly gap since 2009 (Exhibit 4A), while the share of S&P 500 constituents
trading above their 200-day moving average rebounded to 62% from just 43% at the

end of September, and 76% of stocks outperformed the index for the month.
Exhibit 4: Market Leadership Has Been Shifting Beneath the Surface.
A) October Saw a Sharp Broadening in Market Leadership. B) Value Has Quietly Led Growth in 2026.

not possible to invest directly in an index.

The latest rotation is not the first attempt at a change in market leadership this year.
The more important question is what that shift represents: a fundamental reassessment
of the AI trade or another step toward broader, more mature market leadership?
Recent Q3 Technology earnings results have sharpened that debate. Investors are
increasingly looking beyond the scale of AI spending to companies’ ability to translate
that investment into durable growth and attractive returns. The result may be greater

dispersion not only between Growth and Value, but also among AI leaders as investors
become more discerning about which companies can justify the scale of their
investments.
Investment Implications
We continue to look for potential
missing opportunities and favor
diversification across and within asset
classes in a multi-asset portfolio.
In particular, we see potential
opportunities to broaden exposure
beyond AI leaders toward areas
where earnings, capital spending and

productivity trends are improving,
including Value, Small- and Mid-caps,
and select non-U.S. markets, particularly
EMs and Japan. As market leadership
evolves, maintaining diversified
exposures and periodically rebalancing
portfolios can help investors participate
in a broader set of possible return
drivers without becoming overly
dependent on any single theme.

### Page 5

5 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
But greater selectivity does not necessarily signal the end of the AI investment cycle.
YoY growth rates may become harder to maintain after two years of rapid acceleration
as the comparison base rises, but absolute levels of AI capital spending remain elevated.
What matters more is whether spending translates into strong productivity and earnings
growth ahead and whether AI demand continues to support investment. October’s

volatility may therefore reflect less a retreat from the AI theme and more a recalibration
of valuations and expected returns, as other areas of the market begin to contribute
more meaningfully to market performance.
Several developments ahead will help determine whether that rotation has staying
power. One important test is whether broader participation can persist if pressure on AI
stocks continues. Continued strength outside the AI trade would support the case that

investors are reallocating capital rather than broadly reducing risk. The path of monetary
policy will also matter as investors assess where borrowing costs may be headed. The
Federal Reserve’s not-so-hawkish hold in October offered some reassurance, but the
path ahead remains dependent on the evolving balance between inflation and growth.
Any signs of resolution of geopolitical tensions remain an important swing factor
particularly as recent flashpoints in the Middle East have pressured energy prices higher

again. Results of the midterm elections should also provide greater visibility into the
policy landscape for the next few years. While the market does not need every clearing
event to resolve favorably, greater clarity around these uncertainties could help narrow
the range of potential outcomes facing investors.
For investors, October’s sudden change in leadership reinforces the importance of
diversification. Leadership can shift quickly, leaving portfolios that become overly

dependent on a single theme, sector or style particularly vulnerable when it does. We
remain constructive on U.S. Equities and believe that the market may be transitioning
more toward a mid-cycle phase in which returns become broader but also more
measured compared to the sharp acceleration earlier in the cycle. That argues not for
abandoning the market’s long-term winners, but for complementing them with exposure
to additional sources of returns across style, size, sector and region.

### Page 6

6 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Reorienting around AI: From Corporate Plans to Construction to Capital Goods
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
For all the questions (and recent market weakness) hanging over the AI trade, from
monetization and cash burn to financing risk and overcapacity, a measurable aspect is
the buildout itself. Data center construction is lifting construction spending and capital

goods orders, while companies are adding and reviving capacity to meet AI-related
demand. Still, planning delays, access to power, labor scarcity, local politics and the cost
of capital remain meaningful constraints on the buildout’s pace or profitability.
Corporate Plans. Take Caterpillar as one example that has resumed production of a
10-megawatt natural gas turbine discontinued back in 2022. A 175-year old glassmaker,
Corning, is leaning into optical connectivity as AI clusters require faster connections of

optical fiber cabling between servers. Caterpillar and Corning are just two companies
among others pivoting to supply data centers.
Construction to Capital Goods. The AI reorientation is evident in construction
spending as well, where data center construction has outpaced every other major
residential and nonresidential category. Over the last year AI-related construction on data
centers has surged more than 60% while spending on residential, lodging, commercial

or office has declined notably (Exhibit 5A). Data center construction is running at an $85
billion (B) annualized rate this year, versus roughly $49B a year earlier, and now accounts
for 11% of private nonresidential construction, up from just 2% in 2022 according to
the Census Bureau. Similarly, demand for artificial intelligence business equipment and
transportation equipment drove an increase in durable goods orders in September on the
back of new orders for U.S.-manufactured capital goods, suggesting business spending

on equipment remained robust in Q3. Another factor, see Exhibit 5B, where critical
components such as generators, switchboards, chillers and transformers continue to
face long lead times that affect delivery timelines and ultimate profitability for new data
center development.
These signposts reinforce our view that we are only in the middle innings of the AI
investment cycle. From an investment perspective, huge demand for data centers, power
systems, networking infrastructure, specialized chips and other equipment continues

to strain supply, creating both bottlenecks and potential opportunities for companies
powering the buildout.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500/Market-cap Weighted Index is a market-capitalization-weighted index that is widely regarded as the best single gauge of large-cap U.S. equities. The index includes 500 leading

companies and covers approximately 80% of available market capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index includes the same constituents as the capitalization weighted S&P 500, but each company in the S&P 500 EWI is allocated a fixed weight - or 0.2% of the
index total at each quarterly rebalance.
Bloomberg U.S. Aggregate Bond Index is a broad-based, market-capitalization-weighted benchmark that measures the performance of the U.S. investment-grade, U.S. dollar-denominated,

fixed-rate taxable bond market
Bloomberg Commodity Index is a broadly diversified financial benchmark that tracks the price movements of physical commodities through futures contracts.
MSCI Emerging Market Index captures large and mid cap representation across 24 Emerging Markets (EM) countries.
United Nations Food and Agriculture Food Price Index is a measure of the monthly change in international prices of a basket of food commodities.
Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is a market-cap-weighted index focusing on 30 leading semiconductor firms, offering insights into the tech market.

Investing involves risk, including the possible loss of principal. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.

investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 309: COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses

Source PDF: all report pdf/COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Is COP still worth following? We think so. What matters for
businesses is whether this year’s COP31 can improve the
business case for action. Here are four areas to watch

Coco Zhang and Gerben Hieminga
The global climate landscape has changed signiﬁcantly since world
leaders committed 11 years ago to limiting global warming to well
below 2°C and pursuing eﬀorts to limit warming to 1.5°C.
Geopolitical tensions, policy shifts, trade protectionism, rising
energy demand… they have all made it harder for governments to
turn climate commitments into action.
That raises an important question for businesses: Is the Conference
of the Parties (COP) still worth following?
Our answer is yes.
Businesses do not just invest in climate targets; they invest in viable
projects and operations. But many of them still face economic and
practical barriers. Grid constraints and high electricity costs can
slow electriﬁcation. Greener industrial products can struggle to

### Page 2

attract buyers willing to pay a premium. And climate adaptation
plans do not always translate into investable projects.
Businesses cannot solve these challenges alone. In a 2025 UN
survey of more than 2,000 CEOs across 128 countries, 92% said
strong global governance and coordinated policy are “critical” or
“important” to implementing their sustainability agendas.
That is why COP is still relevant. It can help align policy priorities,
improving investment conditions, and connecting businesses with

the right resources. At COP31, four areas will be particularly
important for businesses to watch.
Electriﬁcation marries economic growth
with sustainability
Electriﬁcation is increasingly important for economic growth,
energy security, and sustainability. Demand from AI, EVs, heat
pumps and industrial decarbonisation is accelerating electricity
use, which is growing twice as fast as overall energy demand.
Electricity also punches above its weight economically, accounting

for 23% of global ﬁnal energy consumption but powering 46% of
global GDP. This implies that high value-added sectors rely more on
electricity than the overall economy.
Electricity’s economic contribution
outweighs its share of energy use

Source: ING Research, International Energy Agency
COP31’s push to accelerate electriﬁcation reﬂects this trend. The
COP31 presidency has set a goal of increasing electricity’s share of
ﬁnal energy consumption from 23% today to 35% by 2035.
Getting there will require addressing a major constraint: the grid. As
we have written, grid congestion could slow electriﬁcation, increase
costs, and delay electricity-dependent economic activity.
The key question at COP31 is how governments turn this ambition

into action. We think eﬀective action could include:
Establish a detailed electriﬁcation roadmap with interim
benchmarks to guide progress and sustain momentum across
future COPs
Enhance knowledge-sharing platforms. The International
Smart Grid Action Network has extensive experience sharing
best practices, but could go further by translating knowledge
into direct technical assistance and ready-to-deploy models
Set country- or sector-level targets and pathways to encourage

industry alignment and commitment
Improve access to ﬁnance by expanding dedicated ﬁnancing
for developing countries, while lowering upfront costs in Europe
Improve the economics. Coordinate energy pricing reforms to
make electriﬁcation more competitive with fossil fuel
combustion alternatives, including by reducing taxes and grid
fees that raise electricity prices
Strengthen infrastructure by encouraging governments to
reduce permitting barriers (e.g. Europe and the US), advance

### Page 4

regulatory support for power demand ﬂexibility, and expand
cross-border grids where needed (e.g. Southeast Asia)
If COP31 delivers meaningful action in these areas, the implications
for businesses could be signiﬁcant. Stronger policy could accelerate
near-term grid connections and enhance long-term investment
conﬁdence. Meanwhile, companies may need to work more closely
with utilities and regulators to secure the power they need.
Green industrialisation remains critical

But electriﬁcation is only part of the story. Industry is another
major source of process emissions, with sectors such as steel,
cement, and aviation accounting for roughly 20% of the global
total. Cutting these emissions is particularly diﬃcult because many
industrial processes still have few cost-eﬀective alternatives without
lengthy site overhauls.
COP31 has therefore put green industrialisation high on the
agenda, building on COP30’s progress. Following the Belém

Declaration on Global Green Industrialisation at COP30, a coalition
led by the Brazilian and UK governments mobilised hundreds of
millions of dollars in concessional ﬁnance for Brazilian industries
and awarded funding to 12 hydrogen projects. Over time, these
eﬀorts could reshape manufacturing processes and investment.
But many industrial decarbonisation technologies still face
signiﬁcant barriers to scaling. Their high cost premiums mean
economics depend heavily on government support. Demand for

cleaner industrial products remains limited. And the necessary
infrastructure for many is still underdeveloped.
Hard-to-abate sectors’ green premium is
way higher than what companies are willing
to pay

COP31 is not setting a new level of ambition for green
industrialisation. It’s focusing on implementing the existing
agenda. One priority is narrowing the demand gap. COP31 can
coordinate governments to create more demand for low-carbon
industrial products through long-term public procurement. If such
eﬀorts gain momentum at COP31, they could provide the long-run
revenue certainty investors need to back green industrial projects at

scale.
Climate ﬁnance faces multiple gaps
To realise the targets and priorities set forth at COP, investment is
crucial. And total ﬂows are growing. Global climate ﬁnance reached
around $2tn in 2024 and continued to grow in 2025, although at a
slower rate.
Global climate ﬁnance continues to grow

But the headline number masks signiﬁcant gaps. First, even though
climate mitigation (i.e. emissions reduction) ﬁnance accounted for
$1.9tn of the $2tn total, the level falls well short of estimated
needs, which range from $7.6tn-$9.6tn through 2050. In areas such
as electriﬁcation and green industrialisation, funding is needed not
only for technologies, but also for the infrastructure and demand

needed to scale them.
Second, only $64bn of the total went exclusively to adaptation, an
area requiring surging investment because of intensifying climate
risks. Third, a substantial share of climate ﬁnance stays within
advanced economies or comes from domestic sources.
International ﬂows to help developing countries totalled only
$175bn, far below COP29’s target of $1.3tn per year by 2035.

Resources Defense Council
More climate products and solutions are emerging in the latter two
segments. But their higher risks or weaker business cases can make
it diﬃcult to attract capital without supportive policies. Investors are
actively turning to blended ﬁnance, which uses public capital to
help de-risk projects and mobilise private investment. Yet many still
lack the data, resources and connections needed to move projects

forward. This is where COP31 can help. As we discuss in the next
section, it can help governments create more favourable
investment conditions and connect suppliers, buyers and investors.
Adaptation is becoming a business
imperative and opportunity
Business awareness of climate adaptation has grown, as many
businesses are experiencing more disruptions ﬁrsthand. Our
previous analysis also shows how drought on Europe’s Rhine River,
hurricanes in the US, and other extreme weather events are already

aﬀecting supply chains across sectors. In aggregate, physical
climate risks could cost companies in the S&P Global 1200 index
$1.2tn annually by 2050.
One barrier to corporate investment in climate adaptation is that it
is still seen primarily as a way to avoid losses. But adaptation can
also create business value. Investments in resilient assets, cooling
systems, supplier diversiﬁcation and climate-resilient materials can
improve reliability, competitiveness and long-term growth.

Research from the World Resources Institute suggests that every $1
invested in adaptation can generate up to $10 in beneﬁts. That
return could be even higher for highly climate-exposed sectors. For

### Page 8

example, the World Economic Forum estimates returns of $19–$43
per dollar invested in the food and beverage sector.
COP can help turn adaptation priorities into investable projects.
Since the Paris Agreement, countries have developed National
Adaptation Plans (NAPs), but many remain high-level and lack
speciﬁc, actionable projects. Recent COP eﬀorts have sought to
change this by encouraging governments to translate their NAPs
into portfolios of investable adaptation projects.

But identifying projects does not guarantee ﬁnancing. This is where
COP31 could make a diﬀerence. Its proposed Climate
Implementation Bridge (BRIDGE) initiative could make projects
easier for investors to identify and assess by centralising
information on technical requirements, risks, costs and beneﬁts.
Expanding the initiative to more countries could give more banks,
investors and companies access to potential adaptation
investments.
For businesses, a successful COP31 could therefore help unlock

capital for adaptation projects. The beneﬁts could extend
throughout supply chains, as companies increasingly depend on
the climate resilience of their suppliers to maintain reliable
operations.
Conclusion
The most eﬀective corporate sustainability strategies support long-
term growth, but they are more eﬀective with international
coordination and strong policy support. COP can bring
governments and businesses together to provide such a
framework, even amid geopolitical tensions and concerns over

slowing progress. COP31 may not deliver a landmark global
agreement, but practical advances in these four priorities could still
have immediate relevance for businesses.

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

India: Degrees of rupee defence

16 October 2026 
Radhika Rao, Senior Economist 
radhikarao@dbs.com

### Page 2

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

► 美國經濟仍具韌性 ，惟通膨壓力猶存 。觀察

眾持有聯邦債務占 GDP的比率將達 101%，
財政可持續性風險升溫 。與此同時 ，AI基礎

建設舉債熱潮 ，疊加中東局勢動盪帶來的通
膨不確定性 ，迫使投資人重新定價長天期債
券風險 ，要求更高的風險溢酬 。隨實質殖利
率上行，美債10年期殖利率揚升並持續高懸
於5%水準之上，30年期長債殖利率亦同步站
上近20年來高點 。隨長天期債券利率續揚 ，
殖利率曲線趨陡峭 ，債券配置宜先避開長天
期部位，聚焦中短天期債券鎖利機會。

### Page 6

投資焦點
► 距離美國期中選舉投票僅剩不到1個月，觀察
Polymarket賭盤預估數據顯示 ，眾議院選情
較為明朗，民主黨有超過9成的機率控制眾議
院。但參議院選情則尚有變數 ，今年以來兩
黨均互有領先 ，雖然數據顯示民主黨掌控參
議院機率呈現上揚 ，然隨時序進入選前最後
衝刺期 ，參議院能否亦由民主黨掌控仍待觀
察。故就選前而言 ，由於不同政黨的政策傾
向差異 ，潛在受惠產業亦有不同 ，選舉結果
的不確定性恐令投資人不易大舉押注股市。
► 另一方面 ，油價高檔徘迴 ，導致年底前通 膨
擔憂仍存 ，加上AI企業發債需求成長 、美國
財政體質惡化等 因素共同發酵 ，推動近期 美
債殖利率逐步攀高 ，並令股市預期本益 比壓
力延續 ，進而限縮了投資人短期對美股的追
價動能，短期指數恐維持震盪輪動格局 。可
留意抗波動 、體質佳且具價值優勢之標的 ，
待期中選舉結束與3Q26財報展開後，不確定
性減退將有助資金回歸長線AI題材。
殖利率攀升令美股本益比面臨壓力參議院期中選舉選情仍具變數
期中選前擾動與殖利率走高，美股短期恐仍震盪輪動

投資焦點日股
► 9月日本銀行決議升息 1碼，明確表示潛在
CPI已接近2%，未來也將繼續調高利率 ，政
策重點開始轉向防範通膨向上偏離目標 。然
因有2位委員反對升息，加上總裁植田和男會
後談話偏向中性 ，令市場解讀此次升息未如
預期鷹派，美日利差不易快速縮窄，故9月會
議後日元反而呈現利多出盡的回貶反應。
► 觀察今年以來日股面臨短線日圓升值的狀態
並未出現顯著走弱 態勢。一方面反映企業治
理改革推動股東報酬 提升已成為 支撐股價的
重要結構性因素 。另一方面 ，升息代表日本
經濟已具備一定的健康程度 ，貨幣政策正常
化將不致對需求帶來衝擊 。因此 ，隨BoJ延
續升息方向 ，將擴大銀行淨利差 ，改善其獲
利結構，並提升金融資產收益 。儘管近期整
體日股預期獲利成長走勢略顯停滯 ，然日本
銀行股 在總 體經 濟 與貨幣 政策環 境的 支持
下，獲利成長有望逆勢攀升 ，料將成為後續
資金關注重點。
日圓升值對日股影響似乎有限，關注銀行股獲利走強BoJ升息方向延續，惟其步伐仍取決於經濟與物價

BoJ政策保持正常化路線，日本銀行股有望持續受惠
